###########################
# Annexation Events concerning Europe
###########################
# By lots of folks
# Converted from on_actions to decisions by Rylock and Dr_Njitram
# Any new post-annexation events need to have their triggers in the on_actions file
# Adapted and Expanded by CaptainKaiczer
###########################
### Category ###
# 11xxx = Western Europe
# 12xxx = Southern Europe (Italian Peninsula)
# 13xxx = Central Europe
# 14xxx = Northern Europe
# 15xxx = Eastern Europe (+ Baltic)
# 16xxx = Southern Europe (Balkan)
# 17xxx = Southeastern Europe (Caucasus)
############################

add_namespace = annex

#####################################
###  W E S T E R N   E U R O P E  ###
#####################################

country_event = { # Fate of Great Britain
	id = annex.11001
	title = annexation_britain
	desc = annexation_britain_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_map_britain

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			ENG = { drop_cosmetic_tag = yes }
			event_target:original_britain_puppet = { clear_cores_of_tag_if_possible = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { is_british_tag = yes }
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					tag = IMP
					IMP = {
						NOT = { original_tag = ENG }
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
				IMP = {
					save_event_target_as = british_exiles
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					ENT = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
				}
				ENT = { save_event_target_as = british_exiles }
			}
			388 = { retain_state = yes }
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Great Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11001 option annex.11001.a"
		name = annex.11001.a
		trigger = {
			381 = { is_valid_annexation_state = yes }
			annexations_can_release_united_kingdom = no
			is_subject = no
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:original_britain_puppet }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_great_britain = yes }
			add_core_of = var:tag_to_release
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = {	#Restore United Kingdom
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11001 option annex.11001.b"
		name = annex.11001.b
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_release_united_kingdom = yes
			is_subject = no
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:original_united_kingdom }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_great_britain = yes }
			add_core_of = var:tag_to_release
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Restore England (EGL)
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11001 option annex.11001.c"
		name = annex.11001.c
		trigger = {
			381 = { is_valid_annexation_state = no }
			annexations_can_release_united_kingdom = no
			is_subject = no
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = EGL }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_england = yes }
			add_core_of = var:tag_to_release
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Release England, Scotland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11001 option annex.11001.d"
		name = annex.11001.d
		trigger = {
			381 = { is_valid_annexation_state = yes }
			annexations_can_release_united_kingdom = no
			is_subject = no
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SCO }
		every_state = {
			limit = { region = 3 }
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = EGL }
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_england = yes
					is_wales = yes
				}
			}
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Release England, Wales, Scotland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11001 option annex.11001.e"
		name = annex.11001.e
		trigger = {
			381 = { is_valid_annexation_state = yes }
			annexations_can_release_united_kingdom = no
			is_subject = no
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SCO }
		every_state = {
			limit = { region = 3 }
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = EGL }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_england = yes }
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = WLS }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_wales = yes }
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to British Remnants Ally
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11001 option annex.europe.give_to_british_exiles"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_british_exiles
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = british_exiles
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_major = yes
				NOT = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:british_exiles
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			tag_to_release = event_target:british_exiles
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_great_britain = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give it to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11001 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					region = 1
					region = 2
				}
				is_wales = no
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate Back to Original Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11001 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_great_britain = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11001 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = { recheck_annexations = yes }
}

country_event = { #Fate of Scotland
	id = annex.11002
	title = annexation_scotland
	desc = annexation_scotland_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_map_britain

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			SCO = { clear_cores_of_tag_if_possible = yes }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						377 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes }
						379 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes }
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					377 = {
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = britain
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					379 = {
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = britain
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_event_target = TFH_imperial_leader
					IMP = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						NOT = {
							tag = event_target:britain
							original_tag = ENG
						}
					}
				}
				IMP = { save_event_target_as = british_exiles }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {	#Puppet Scotland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11002 option annex.11002.a"
		name = annex.11002.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:british_exiles }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_event_target = britain
				OR = {
					is_major = no
					is_ally_with = event_target:britain
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_event_target = british_exiles
				is_major = no
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SCO }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_scotland = yes }
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11002 option annex.give_to_britain"
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = britain
			NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:british_exiles }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_major = yes
				OR = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:britain }
					event_target:britain = {
						is_subject = yes
						NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 0
				NOT = { has_event_target = british_exiles }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:britain = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:britain }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_scotland = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to British Exiles
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11002 option annex.give_to_british_exiles"
		name = annex.give_to_british_exiles
		trigger = { has_event_target = british_exiles }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:british_exiles = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:british_exiles }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_scotland = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to Overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11002 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = { annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_scotland = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	option = { #Integrate Back to Original Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11002 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = { annexations_can_annex = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { is_british_tag = yes }
			every_state = {
				limit = { is_scotland = yes }
				remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			every_state = {
				limit = { is_scotland = yes }
				add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Cancel Annexation
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11002 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	after = { recheck_annexations = yes }
}

country_event = { #Fate of Wales
	id = annex.11003
	title = annexation_wales
	desc = annexation_wales_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_map_britain

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			WLS = { clear_cores_of_tag_if_possible = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { 375 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } }
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					375 = {
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = britain
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_event_target = TFH_imperial_leader
					event_target:TFH_imperial_leader = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						NOT = {
							tag = event_target:britain
							original_tag = ENG
						}
					}
				}
				event_target:TFH_imperial_leader = { save_event_target_as = british_exiles }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet Wales
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11003 option annex.11003.a"
		name = annex.11003.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:british_exiles }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_event_target = britain
				OR = {
					is_major = no
					is_ally_with = event_target:britain
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_event_target = british_exiles
				is_major = no
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = WLS }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_wales = yes }
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11003 option annex.give_to_britain"
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = britain
			NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:british_exiles }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_major = yes
				OR = {
					NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:britain }
					event_target:britain = {
						is_subject = yes
						NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 0
				NOT = { has_event_target = british_exiles }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:britain = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:britain }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_wales = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to British Exiles
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11003 option annex.give_to_british_exiles"
		name = annex.give_to_british_exiles
		trigger = { has_event_target = british_exiles }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:british_exiles = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:british_exiles }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_wales = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to Overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11003 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = { annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_wales = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate Back to Original Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11003 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = { annexations_can_annex = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { is_british_tag = yes }
			every_state = {
				limit = { is_wales = yes }
				remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			every_state = {
				limit = { is_wales = yes }
				add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel Annexation
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11003 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	after = { recheck_annexations = yes }
}

country_event = { #Fate of Northern Ireland
	id = annex.11004
	title = annexation_northern_ireland
	desc = annexation_northern_ireland_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_map_britain

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = IRE
						AND = {
							annexations_britain_can_annex_overseas = yes
							owns_state = 380
						}
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = { IRE = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
				IRE = { save_event_target_as = ireland }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					6 = {
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:ireland }
							owns_state = 380
							has_socialist_government = no
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:6.owner = { save_event_target_as = britain }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to Ireland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11004 option annex.europe.give_to_ireland"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_ireland
		trigger = { has_event_target = ireland }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:ireland }
				is_ally_with = event_target:britain
				annexations_AI_will_consider_returning_colonies = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:ireland = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:ireland }
		388 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes }
	}

	option = { #Give to Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11004 option annex.europe.give_to_britain"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_britain
		trigger = { has_event_target = britain }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_will_consider_returning_colonies = no
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:britain }
				OR = {
					is_major = yes
					is_ally_with = event_target:ireland
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:britain = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:britain }
		388 = { #Northern Ireland
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
			add_claim_by = var:tag_to_release
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11004 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = { annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		388 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes }		
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11004 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = { annexations_can_annex = yes }
		388 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes }		
	}
	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11004 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	after = { recheck_annexations = yes }
}

country_event = { #Fate of Ireland
	id = annex.11005
	title = annexation_ireland
	desc = annexation_ireland_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_map_ireland
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			388 = {
				add_core_of = IRE
				retain_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Puppet Ireland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11005 option annex.11005.a"
		name = annex.11005.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			is_british_tag = no
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = IRE }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Create United Ireland : Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11005 option annex.11005.b"
		name = annex.11005.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			is_british_tag = yes
		}
		388 = {
			#Northern Ireland
			if = {
				limit = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				remove_claim_by = ROOT
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				remove_core_of = ROOT
			}
			clear_retain_state = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = IRE }
		var:tag_to_release = {
			every_core_state = {
				limit = { is_valid_annexation_state = yes }
				transfer_state_without_overriding_occupation = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Create Ireland without Northern Ireland : Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11005 option annex.11005.c"
		name = annex.11005.c
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			is_british_tag = yes
			owns_state = 388
		}

country_event = { #Fate of France
	id = annex.11006
	title = annexation_france
	desc = annexation_france_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_map_france

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { country_exists = FRA } }
				FRA = { clear_cores_of_tag_if_possible = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { country_exists = FPP } }
				FPP = { clear_cores_of_tag_if_possible = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_french_tag = yes }
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_french_tag = no
					is_subject = no
				}
				random_other_country = {
					limit = {
						is_french_tag = yes
						is_ally_with = ROOT
					}
					save_event_target_as = france
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = france } }
				if = {
					limit = { FRA = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
					FRA = { save_event_target_as = france }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { FPP = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
					FPP = { save_event_target_as = france }
				}
			}
			68 = { retain_state = yes }	#Pas-de-Calais
			71 = { retain_state = yes }	#Alsace-Lorraine
			77 = { retain_state = yes } #Brittany
			83 = { retain_state = yes } #Basses-Pyrenees
			84 = { retain_state = yes }	#Roussillon
			94 = { retain_state = yes } #Savoy
			95 = { retain_state = yes } #Nice
		}
	}

	option = { #Restore the French Republic
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11006 option annex.11006.b"
		name = annex.11006.b
		trigger = {
			is_ally_with = ENT
			NOT = { country_exists = FRA }
			is_french_tag = no
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_release_puppet_default_logic = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = FRA }
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_france = yes
				NOT = { is_core_of = var:tag_to_release }
			}
			add_claim_by = var:tag_to_release
		}
		release_targeted_tag_with_claims = yes
	}

	option = { #Set up a French Puppet Government
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11006 option annex.11006.c"
		name = annex.11006.c
		trigger = {
			is_french_tag = no
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { country_exists = FPP }
			OR = {
				NOT = { is_ally_with = ENT }
				FRA = { 
					exists = yes
					NOT = { is_ally_with = ROOT }
				}
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:france }
				event_target:france = {
					is_subject = yes
					NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_release_puppet_default_logic = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = FPP }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_france = yes }
			add_core_of = var:tag_to_release
			hidden_effect = { remove_claim_by = var:tag_to_release }
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11006 option annex.give_to_france"
		name = annex.give_to_france
		trigger = { has_event_target = france }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				event_target:france = { annexations_AI_grant_land_default_logic = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:france }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_france = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11006 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = { annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_france = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11006 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = { annexations_can_annex = yes }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_france = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}
	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11006 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	after = { recheck_annexations = yes }
}

		161 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Aosta Valley
	}

country_event = { #Fate of Corsica
	id = annex.11014
	title = annexation_corsica
	desc = annexation_corsica_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_map_france

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = FRA
						91 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Provence
						92 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Languedoc
						133 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Liguria
						130 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Tuscany
						4 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Latium
						126 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Campania
						2 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Sardinia
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					91 = { #Provence
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
					}
				}
				var:91.owner = { save_event_target_as = france }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					92 = { #Languedoc
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
					}
				}
				var:92.owner = { save_event_target_as = france }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					FRA = {
						NOT = { tag = event_target:france }
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
				FRA = { save_event_target_as = france_exile }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					133 = { #Liguria
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
					}
				}
				var:133.owner = { save_event_target_as = italy_piedmont }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					130 = { #Tuscany
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:italy_piedmont }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:130.owner = { save_event_target_as = italy_tuscany }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					4 = { #Latium
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:italy_piedmont }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:italy_tuscany }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:4.owner = { save_event_target_as = italy_latium }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					126 = { #Campania
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:italy_piedmont }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:italy_tuscany }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:italy_latium }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:126.owner = { save_event_target_as = italy_campania }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					2 = { #Sardinia
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:italy_piedmont }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:italy_tuscany }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:italy_latium }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:italy_campania }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:2.owner = { save_event_target_as = italy_sardinia }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_ally_with = event_target:france
						is_ally_with = event_target:france_exile
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = has_french_ally
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_ally_with = event_target:italy_piedmont
						is_ally_with = event_target:italy_tuscany
						is_ally_with = event_target:italy_latium
						is_ally_with = event_target:italy_campania
						is_ally_with = event_target:italy_sardinia
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = has_italian_ally
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give it to France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11014 option annex.give_to_france"
		name = annex.give_to_france
		trigger = { has_event_target = france }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}

				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:france }
				has_event_target = has_french_ally
				is_germany_or_splinter_tag = no
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:france }
				has_event_target = has_italian_ally
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1

			}

country_event = { #Fate of Spain
	id = annex.11015
	title = annexation_spain
	desc = annexation_spain_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_spain = no }
					remove_core_of = SPA
				}
				else = {
					add_core_of = SPA
				}
				# Civil War Spains Core-Claim Removal
				# remove_core_of = SPR
				# remove_core_of = SWF
				remove_claim_by = SPA
				# remove_claim_by = SPR
				# remove_claim_by = SWF
			}
			96 = { retain_state = yes } #Catalonia
			97 = { retain_state = yes } #Balearic Islands
			98 = { retain_state = yes } #Valencia
			104 = { retain_state = yes } #Navarre
			105 = { retain_state = yes } #Basque Country
			115 = { retain_state = yes } #Galicia
			121 = { retain_state = yes } #Gibraltar
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet Spain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11015 option annex.11015.a"
		name = annex.11085.a
		trigger = { is_subject = no }
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SPA }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11015 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = { annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		SPA = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11015 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {

	}

country_event = { #Fate of Gibraltar
	id = annex.11019
	title = annexation_gibraltar
	desc = annexation_gibraltar_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						169 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Seville
						173 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Málaga
						annexations_britain_can_annex_overseas = yes
						annexations_AI_wants_to_own_gibraltar = yes
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { annexations_major_can_annex_strategic_territories = yes }
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
				save_event_target_as = AI_dont_annex
			}
			annexations_create_non_socialist_britain_event_target = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					114 = { #Seville
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:britain }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:114.owner = { save_event_target_as = spain_seville }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					100 = { #Málaga
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:britain }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:spain_seville }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:100.owner = { save_event_target_as = spain_malaga }
			}
		}
	}

				factor = 0

			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:spain_seville }
				is_ally_with = event_target:spain_malaga
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:spain_seville }
				has_event_target = britain
				annexations_AI_will_consider_returning_colonies = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = spain_malaga }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:spain_seville = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:spain_seville }
		121 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Gibraltar
	}

	option = { #Give to Spanish owner of Málaga

country_event = { #Fate of Portugal
	id = annex.11020
	title = annexation_portugal
	desc = annexation_portugal_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { country_exists = POR } }
				every_state = {
					if = {
						limit = { is_portugal = no }
						remove_core_of = POR
					}
					else = {
						add_core_of = POR
					}
					remove_claim_by = POR
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = SPA
					has_socialist_government = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					SPA = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						OR = {
							is_subject = no
							has_socialist_government = yes
						}
					}
				}
				SPA = { save_event_target_as = spain }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Liberate a republican Portugal
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11020 option annex.11020.a"
		name = annex.11020.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { country_exists = POR }
				OR = {
					is_monarchy = yes
					is_german_tag = yes
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_ally_with = event_target:spain
				OR = {
					is_major = no
					has_socialist_government = yes
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = POR }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	# option = { #Restore the Portuguese monarchy
	# 	log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11020 option annex.11020.b"
	# 	name = annex.11020.b
	# 	trigger = {
	# 		is_subject = no
	# 		NOT = { country_exists = POR }
	# 		has_socialist_government = no
	# 	}
	# 	ai_chance = {
	# 		base = 50
	# 		modifier = {
	# 			factor = 0
	# 			annexations_can_annex = yes
	# 		}
	# 		modifier = {
	# 			factor = 0
	# 			is_monarchy = no
	# 			is_german_tag = no
	# 		}
	# 		modifier = {
	# 			factor = 0
	# 			is_ally_with = event_target:spain
	# 			is_major = no
	# 		}
	# 	}
	# 	set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = POR }
	# 	release_targeted_tag = yes
	# 	var:tag_to_release = { POR_monarchy_setup = yes }
	# }

	option = { #give to Spain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11020 option annex.give_to_spain"
		name = annex.give_to_spain
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = spain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_major = yes
				has_socialist_government = no
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:spain }
			}
			modifier = {

				event_target:spain = { is_subject = yes }

		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

country_event = { #Fate of Limburg
	id = annex.11023
	title = annexation_limburg
	desc = annexation_limburg_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						61 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #North Brabant
						12 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Brabant
						51 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Western Rhineland
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
				if = {
					limit = { is_germany_or_splinter_tag = yes }
					save_event_target_as = AI_dont_annex
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					61 = { #North Brabant
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
					}
				}
				var:61.owner = { save_event_target_as = netherlands }
			}
			if = {

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			annexations_reset_belgian_tags = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_belgian_tag = yes
						69 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Picardy
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_belgian_tag = no
						has_any_authoritarian_government = no
					}
					save_event_target_as = AI_dont_annex
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					var:12.owner = { #Brabant
						is_belgian_tag = yes
						NOT = { is_subject_of = HOL }
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
				var:12.owner = { save_event_target_as = flanders }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					69 = { #Picardy
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
					}
				}
				var:69.owner = { save_event_target_as = france }
			}
			66 = { retain_state = yes } #Eupen
			64 = { retain_state = yes } #Limburg
			62 = { retain_state = yes } #Westerscheldt
		}
	}

	option = { #puppet Belgium
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11026 option annex.11026.a"
		name = annex.11026.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				63 = { is_valid_annexation_state = yes } #Flanders
				12 = { is_valid_annexation_state = yes } #Brabant
			}
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_monarchy = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
				OR = {
					is_belgian_tag = yes
					has_any_authoritarian_government = yes
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_event_target = flanders
				OR = {
					is_major = no
					is_ally_with = event_target:flanders
					event_target:flanders = { has_socialist_government = yes }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_event_target = france
				OR = {
					is_major = no
					is_ally_with = event_target:france
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = BEL }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		# var:tag_to_release = { BEL_puppet_setup = yes }
	}

	option = { #puppet Wallonia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11026 option annex.11026.c"
		name = annex.11026.c
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = WLL }
		65 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Wallonia
		66 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Eupen
		67 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Bastogne
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to Flanders
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.11026 option annex.11026.d"
		name = annex.11026.d
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = flanders
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				event_target:flanders = { is_french_tag = yes }
				is_germany_or_splinter_tag = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:flanders }
				is_ally_with = event_target:france
				OR = {
					is_germany_or_splinter_tag = no
					event_target:france = { is_french_tag = no }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:flanders = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:flanders }
		65 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Wallonia
		67 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Bastogne
	}

	option = { #Give to France

		name = annex.give_to_france
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = france
		}

country_event = { # Fate of Rome
	id = annex.12001
	title = annexation_rome
	desc = annexation_rome_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			annexations_reset_italian_tags = yes
			if = {
				limit = {  
					OR = {
						original_tag = ITA
						has_country_flag = is_potential_unifier_ITA
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = { 
				limit = {
					is_italian_tag = no
					is_subject = no
				}
				random_other_country = {
					limit = {
						is_italian_tag = yes
						OR = {
							is_ally_with = ROOT
							is_subject_of = ROOT
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = italy
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					tag = ENT
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
				random_other_country = {
					limit = {
						is_italian_tag = yes
						OR = {
							is_ally_with = ROOT
							is_subject_of = ROOT
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = italy
				}
			}
			138 = { retain_state = yes }
			149 = { retain_state = yes }
			150 = { retain_state = yes }
			151 = { retain_state = yes }
			155 = { retain_state = yes }
		}
	}

	option = { #Release United Italy
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12001 option annex.12001.a"
		name = annex.12001.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = { country_exists = ITA }	
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ITA }
		annexation_release_italy = yes
	}

	option = { #Release Papal States
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12001 option annex.12001.b"
		name = annex.12001.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = { country_exists = STE }
			NOT = { tag = ENT }
			annexations_can_release_italian_splinters = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = STE }
		4 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes }
		128 = { retain_state = yes } #Umbria
		129 = { retain_state = yes } #Marche
		131 = { retain_state = yes } #Romagna
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to Italy
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12001 option annex.give_to_italy"
		name = annex.give_to_italy
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = italy
		}
		annexations_give_italy_to_target = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12001 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		4 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Latium
	}

	option = { #Annex
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12001 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		4 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Latium
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12001 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

		}

	option = { #Cancel

		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_event_target = italy }
					123 = { #Sicily
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:britain }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:123.owner = { save_event_target_as = italy }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #puppet Malta
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12004 option annex.12004.a"
		name = annex.12004.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = MLT }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12004 option annex.give_to_britain"
		name = annex.give_to_britain
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = britain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_will_consider_returning_colonies = no
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:britain }
				is_ally_with = event_target:italy
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				event_target:britain = { has_socialist_government = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:britain = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:britain }
		122 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Malta
	}

	option = { #Give to Italy
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12004 option annex.give_to_italy"
		name = annex.give_to_italy
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = italy
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:italy }
				is_ally_with = event_target:britain
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				has_event_target = italy
				event_target:italy = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = italy }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:italy }
			122 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Malta
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12004 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		122 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Malta
	}

	option = { #Annex
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12004 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		122 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Malta
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12004 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Sardinia
	id = annex.12005
	title = annexation_sardinia
	desc = annexation_sardinia_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			annexations_reset_italian_tags = yes
			if = {
				limit = {  
					OR = {
						original_tag = ITA
						has_country_flag = is_potential_unifier_ITA
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 
					is_italian_tag = no
					is_subject = no
				}
				random_other_country = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							tag = ITA
							has_country_flag = is_potential_unifier_ITA
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = italy
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Release as SAR
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12005 option annex.12005.a"
		name = annex.12005.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = { country_exists = SAR }
			annexations_can_release_italian_splinters = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SAR }
		2 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Sardinia
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to Italy
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12005 option annex.give_to_italy"
		name = annex.give_to_italy
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = italy
		}
		annexations_give_italy_to_target = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12005 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		2 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Sardinia
	}

	option = { #Annex
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12005 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		2 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Sardinia
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.12005 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Trentino
	id = annex.12009
	title = annexation_trentino
	desc = annexation_trentino_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes 

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						136 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Lombardy
						137 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Venice
						AND = {
							143 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes }
							owns_state = 139
						}
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
				if = {
					limit = { is_germany_or_splinter_tag = yes }
					save_event_target_as = AI_dont_annex
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					136 = {
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
					}
				}
				var:136.owner = { save_event_target_as = lombardy }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					137 = {
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:lombardy }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:137.owner = { save_event_target_as = italy_venice }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					143 = {
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes

								tag = event_target:lombardy
								tag = event_target:italy_venice

				factor = 0

				factor = 0.1

country_event = { # Fate of Switzerland
	id = annex.13001
	title = annexation_switzerland
	desc = annexation_switzerland_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_switzerland = yes }
					add_core_of = SWI
				}
				else = {
					remove_core_of = SWI
				}
				remove_claim_by = SWI
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					49 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes }
					has_country_flag = GER_pan_germany
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					49 = { #Baden
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
					}
				}
				var:49.owner = { save_event_target_as = germany }
			}
			73 = { retain_state = yes } #Geneva
			141 = { retain_state = yes } #Ticino
			142 = { retain_state = yes } #Vorarlberg
		}
	}

	option = { #liberate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13001 option annex.13001.a"
		name = annex.13001.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}

			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes

				factor = 0.4
				has_any_authoritarian_government = yes

				event_target:germany = { is_subject = no }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:germany = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		140 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Zurich
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord

			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		13 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Bern
		140 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Zurich
	}

	option = { #annex

		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = SWI
						75 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Burgundy
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = { SWI = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
				SWI = { save_event_target_as = switzerland }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					75 = { #Burgundy
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:switzerland }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:75.owner = { save_event_target_as = france }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to Switzerland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13002 option annex.europe.give_to_switzerland"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_switzerland
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = switzerland
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_french_tag = yes
				has_any_authoritarian_government = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:switzerland }
				is_ally_with = event_target:france
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:switzerland = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:switzerland }
		73 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Romandy
	}

	option = { #Give to France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13002 option annex.give_to_france"
		name = annex.give_to_france
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = france
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				original_tag = SWI
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:france }
				is_ally_with = event_target:switzerland
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:france = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:france }
		73 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Romandy
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13002 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		73 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Romandy
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13002 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_event_target = switzerland
				has_any_authoritarian_government = no
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { original_tag = SWI }
			73 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Romandy
		}
		else = {
			73 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Romandy
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13002 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Austria
	id = annex.13005
	title = annexation_austria 
	desc = annexation_austria_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_austria = yes }
					add_core_of = AUS
				}
				else = {
					remove_core_of = AUS
					remove_claim_by = AUS
				}	
			}
			139 = { retain_state = yes } #South Tyrol
			147 = { retain_state = yes } #Burgenland
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						57 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Upper Bavaria
						has_country_flag = GER_pan_germany
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					57 = { #Upper Bavaria
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
					}
				}
				var:57.owner = { save_event_target_as = bavaria }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #liberate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13005 option annex.13005.a"
		name = annex.13005.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = AUS }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to owner of Upper Bavaria
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13005 option annex.13005.b"
		name = annex.13005.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = bavaria
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:bavaria }
		AUS = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13005 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		AUS = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #annex
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13005 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_austria = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
			enable_state_integration_50_compliance = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13005 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

		}

	option = { #annex

		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { #Non-Ultranationalist Italy doesn't want South Tyrol
				factor = 0
				is_italian_tag = yes
				NOT = { has_government = ultranationalism }
			}
		}
		139 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #South Tyrol
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13007 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Brandenburg
	id = annex.13008
	title = annexation_brandenburg
	desc = annexation_brandenburg_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			annexations_reset_german_tags = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_german_unifier_tag = yes
						original_tag = NGM
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			annexations_create_german_event_targets = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					annexations_germany_is_independent_ally_or_puppet_of_ROOT = no
					NGM = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
				}
				NGM = { save_event_target_as = north_germany }
			}
			38 = { retain_state = yes } #Holstein
			39 = { retain_state = yes } #Schleswig
		}
	}

	option = { #release Germany
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13008 option annex.europe.release_germany"
		name = annex.europe.release_germany
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_release_germany = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_major = no
				has_event_target = germany
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_event_target = north_germany
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_germany = no
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_in_europe = yes
			}
		}
		annexations_release_germany = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to Germany
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13008 option annex.europe.give_to_germany"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_germany
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = germany
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:germany }
				OR = {
					is_major = yes
					is_ally_with = event_target:north_germany
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:germany = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:germany }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_brandenburg = yes
					is_saxony = yes
				}
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to NGM
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13008 option annex.13008.a"
		name = annex.13008.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = north_germany
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_germany = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:north_germany }
				is_ally_with = event_target:germany
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:north_germany = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:north_germany }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_brandenburg = yes
					is_saxony = yes
				}

			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_brandenburg = yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13008 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_state = {

				OR = {
					is_brandenburg = yes

country_event = { # Fate of Rhineland
	id = annex.13010
	title = annexation_rhineland
	desc = annexation_rhineland_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			annexations_reset_german_tags = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_german_unifier_tag = yes
						41 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #South Hanover
						47 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Thuringia
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			annexations_create_german_event_targets = yes
			if = {
				limit = { annexations_germany_is_independent_ally_or_puppet_of_ROOT = no }
				if = {
					limit = {
						41 = { #South Hanover
							annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
							owner = {
								NOT = { tag = event_target:germany }
								ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
							}
						}
					}
					var:41.owner = { save_event_target_as = south_hanover }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						47 = { #Thuringia
							annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
							owner = {
								NOT = { tag = event_target:germany }
								NOT = { tag = event_target:south_hanover }
								ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
							}
						}
					}

		}
	}

			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_in_europe = yes
			}
		}
		annexations_release_germany = yes
	}

		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13010 option annex.13010.a"
		name = annex.13010.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				annexations_can_release_german_splinters = yes
				is_french_tag = yes
			}
		}

			base = 0

				factor = 100

			}

			limit = { is_rhineland = yes }

country_event = { # Fate of Poland
	id = annex.13016
	title = annexation_poland
	desc = annexation_poland_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_lesser_poland = yes }
					add_core_of = POL
				}
				else = { 
					remove_core_of = POL
					remove_claim_by = POL
				}
			}
			218 = { retain_state = yes } #Bialystok
			220 = { retain_state = yes } #Chelm
			221 = { retain_state = yes } #Lwow
			if = {
				limit = { var:34.owner = { is_german_unifier_tag = yes } }
				211 = { retain_state = yes } #Posen
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #liberate POL
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13016 option annex.13016.a"
		name = annex.13016.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					original_tag = RUS
					original_tag = SOV
				}
				has_socialist_government = no
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = POL }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13016 option annexation_events.give_to_overlord"
		name = annexation_events.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		POL = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #annex
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13016 option annexation_events.integration"
		name = annexation_events.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_event_target = AI_dont_annex
			}
		}
		POL = { add_claim_of_ROOT_on_valid_cores = yes }
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13016 option annexation_events.cancel"
		name = annexation_events.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Czechia
	id = annex.13029
	title = annexation_czechia
	desc = annexation_czechia_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_czechia = yes }
					add_core_of = CZE
				}
				else = { 
					remove_core_of = CZE
				}
				remove_claim_by = CZE
			}
			196 = { retain_state = yes } #Southern Slovakia
			198 = { retain_state = yes } #Subcarpathian Rus'
			209 = { retain_state = yes } #Tesin
		}
	}

	option = { #Liberate Czechoslovakia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13029 option annex.13029.a"
		name = annex.13029.a
		trigger = {
			26 = { is_valid_annexation_state = yes }
			is_subject = no 
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 33
				has_authoritarian_government = no
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = CZE }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_czechoslovakia = yes }
			add_core_of = var:tag_to_release
			hidden_effect = { remove_claim_by = var:tag_to_release }
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		var:tag_to_release = { set_cosmetic_tag = CZSL }
	}

	option = { #Balkanize to Czechia and Slovakia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13029 option annex.13029.b"
		name = annex.13029.b
		trigger = {
			26 = { is_valid_annexation_state = yes }
			is_subject = no 
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1
				has_authoritarian_government = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = CZE }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_owned_state = { is_slovakia = yes }
				26 = { is_valid_annexation_state = yes }
				NOT = { country_exists = SLO }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SLO }
			every_state = {
				limit = { is_slovakia = yes }
				add_core_of = var:tag_to_release
			}
			release_targeted_tag = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Liberate Czechia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13029 option annex.13029.c"
		name = annex.13029.c
		trigger = {
			26 = { is_valid_annexation_state = no }
			is_subject = no 
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				has_authoritarian_government = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = CZE }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13029 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_czechia = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13029 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_czechia = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13029 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Slovakia
	id = annex.13030
	title = annexation_slovakia
	desc = annexation_slovakia_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_slovakia = yes }
					add_core_of = SLO
				}
				else = { 
					remove_core_of = SLO
				}
				remove_claim_by = SLO
			}
			every_country = { #Dissolve Czechoslovakia
				limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = CZSL }
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			}
		}
		196 = { retain_state = yes } #Southern Slovakia
		198 = { retain_state = yes } #Subcarpathian Rus'
		if = {
			limit = { 
				OR = {
					original_tag = CZE
					has_country_flag = ZPS_zapadoslavia
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:8.owner = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = CZE
						has_country_flag = ZPS_zapadoslavia
					}
					ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
				}
			}
			var:8.owner = { save_event_target_as = czechia }
		}
	}

	option = { #Liberate Slovakia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13030 option annex.13030.a"
		name = annex.13030.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no 
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_ally_with = CZE
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { is_ally_with = CZE }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SLO }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_slovakia = yes }
			add_core_of = var:tag_to_release
			hidden_effect = { remove_claim_by = var:tag_to_release }
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to Czechia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13030 option annex.europe.give_to_czechia"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_czechia
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = czechia
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0 
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:czechia }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_slovakia = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
		var:tag_to_release = {
			if = {
				limit = { original_tag = CZE }
				set_cosmetic_tag = CZSL
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13030 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_slovakia = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13030 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_slovakia = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = { 
					original_tag = CZE
					has_country_flag = ZPS_zapadoslavia
				}
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = { is_slovakia = yes }
				remove_claim_and_add_core_of_ROOT = yes
			}
			set_cosmetic_tag = CZSL
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.13030 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Greenland
	id = annex.14003
	title = annexation_greenland
	desc = annexation_greenland_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						17 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes }
						18 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes }
						is_american_tag = yes
						tag = CAN
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							tag = CAN
							is_american_tag = yes
						}
						OR = {
							is_ally_with = event_target:denmark
							is_ally_with = event_target:norway
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = AI_dont_annex
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					17 = { #Denmark
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
					}
				}
				var:17.owner = { save_event_target_as = denmark }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					18 = { #Norway
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = { 
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
							NOT = { tag = event_target:denmark }
						}
					}
				}
				var:18.owner = { save_event_target_as = norway }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { CAN = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
				CAN = { save_event_target_as = canada }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					960 = { #Maryland
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
							NOT = { tag = event_target:denmark }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:norway }
							NOT = { tag = event_target:canada }
						}
					}
				}
				var:960.owner = { save_event_target_as = united_states }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Give to Denmark
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.14003 option annex.europe.give_to_denmark"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_denmark
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = denmark
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						tag = CAN
						is_american_tag = yes
					}
					NOT = {
						is_ally_with = event_target:denmark
						is_ally_with = event_target:norway
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:denmark }
				is_ally_with = event_target:norway
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:denmark = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:denmark }
		368 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Greenland
		369 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Sermersooq
	}

	option = { # Give to Norway
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.14003 option annex.europe.give_to_norway"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_norway
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = norway
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {

country_event = { # Fate of Norway
	id = annex.14004
	title = annexation_norway
	desc = annexation_norway_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_norway = no }
					remove_core_of = NOR
				}
				else = {
					add_core_of = NOR
				}
				remove_claim_by = NOR
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = FIN }
				add_state_claim = 366 #Finnmark
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Liberate Norway
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.14004 option annex.14004.a"
		name = annex.14004.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no 
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = NOR }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.14004 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_norway = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.14004 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_norway = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.14004 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

		361 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #North Schleswig

country_event = { # Fate of Estonia
	id = annex.15001
	title = annexation_estonia
	desc = annexation_estonia_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_estonia = no }
					remove_core_of = EST
				}
				else = {
					add_core_of = EST
				}
				remove_claim_by = EST
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_russian_tag = yes }
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
				save_event_target_as = AI_dont_annex
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Liberate Estonia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.15001 option annex.15001.a"
		name = annex.15001.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no 
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = EST }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.15001 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_estonia = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.15001 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_estonia = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.15001 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes 
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Russia
	id = annex.15004 
	title = annexation_russia
	desc = annexation_russia_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { country_exists = SOV } }
				SOV = { clear_cores_of_tag_if_possible = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { country_exists = RSP } }
				RSP = { clear_cores_of_tag_if_possible = yes }
			}
			if = { 
				limit = { NOT = { country_exists = RUS } } 
				RUS = { clear_cores_of_tag_if_possible = yes }
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = { is_russia = yes }
				add_core_of = RSP 
			}
			225 = { retain_state = yes } #Latgale
			249 = { retain_state = yes } #Gomel
			250 = { retain_state = yes } #Siveria
			261 = { retain_state = yes } #Crimea
			262 = { retain_state = yes } #Vitebsk
			263 = { retain_state = yes } #Krichev
			265 = { retain_state = yes } #Smolensk
			269 = { retain_state = yes } #Rostov
			284 = { retain_state = yes } #Krasnodar
			285 = { retain_state = yes } #Maikov
			296 = { retain_state = yes } #Terek
			306 = { retain_state = yes } #Petrozavodsk
			308 = { retain_state = yes } #Eastern Karelia
			309 = { retain_state = yes } #Soroka
			310 = { retain_state = yes } #Murmansk
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_russian_far_east = yes 
						is_siberia = yes
						is_central_asia = yes
						is_northern_caucasus = yes
					}
				}
				retain_state = yes
			} 
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Liberate Russia 
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.15004 option annex.15004.a"

			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = RSP }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.15004 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord

			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }

country_event = { # Fate of Belarus
	id = annex.15005
	title = annexation_belarus
	desc = annexation_belarus_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_russian_tag = yes }
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = { 
				limit = { NOT = { country_exists = BLR } } 
				BLR = { clear_cores_of_tag_if_possible = yes }
			}
			if = { 
				limit = { NOT = { country_exists = BSR } } 
				BSR = { clear_cores_of_tag_if_possible = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = belarus } }
				if = {
					limit = { BLR = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
					BLR = { save_event_target_as = belarus }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { BSR = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
					BSR = { save_event_target_as = belarus }
				}
			}
			249 = { retain_state = yes } #Gomel
		}
	}

		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_socialist_government = no
			is_belarusian_tag = no 
			NOT = { country_exists = BLR }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:belarus }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = BLR }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_belarus = yes }
			add_core_of = var:tag_to_release
		}

		}

			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:belarus }
			}

			add_core_of = var:tag_to_release
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to Belarus
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.15005 option annex.give_to_belarus"
		name = annex.give_to_belarus
		trigger = { has_event_target = belarus }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				event_target:belarus = { annexations_AI_grant_land_default_logic = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:belarus }

				is_belarus = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes

			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		DAG = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.15007 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_northern_caucasus = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.15007 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
}
