country_event = { # Fate of Serbia
	id = annex.16001
	title = annexation_serbia
	desc = annexation_serbia_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			annexations_reset_yugoslav_tags = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = YUG
						has_country_flag = is_potential_unifier_YUG
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 
					is_yugoslavian_tag = no
					is_subject = no
				}
				random_other_country = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							tag = YUG
							has_country_flag = is_potential_unifier_YUG
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = yugoslavia
				}
			}
			178 = { retain_state = yes } #Kosovo
			180 = { retain_state = yes } #Pirot
			191 = { retain_state = yes } #West Banat
			193 = { retain_state = yes } #Bachka
		}
	}

	option = { # Release YUG
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16001 option annex.16001.a"
		name = annex.16001.a
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_release_yugoslavia = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = YUG }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_yugoslavia = yes }
			add_core_of = var:tag_to_release
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		annexations_merge_yugoslav_subjects = yes
	}
	
	option = { # Release SER
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16001 option annex.16001.b"
		name = annex.16001.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 65
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SER }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}
	
	option = { # Give to YUG 
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16001 option annex.europe.give_to_yugoslavia"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_yugoslavia
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = yugoslavia
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:yugoslavia
					is_in_faction_with = event_target:yugoslavia
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:yugoslavia }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_serbia = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16001 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		SER = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16001 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_serbia = yes
			}
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16001 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			annexations_reset_yugoslav_tags = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = YUG
						has_country_flag = is_potential_unifier_YUG
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 
					is_yugoslavian_tag = no
					is_subject = no
				}
				random_other_country = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							tag = YUG
							has_country_flag = is_potential_unifier_YUG
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = yugoslavia
				}
			}
			155 = { retain_state = yes }
			192 = { retain_state = yes }
		}
	}

	option = { # Release YUG
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16002 option annex.16002.a"
		name = annex.16002.a
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_release_yugoslavia = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = YUG }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_yugoslavia = yes }
			add_core_of = var:tag_to_release
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		annexations_merge_yugoslav_subjects = yes
	}
	
	option = { # Release CRO
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16002 option annex.16002.b"
		name = annex.16002.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 65
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = CRO }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}
	
	option = { # Give to YUG 
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16002 option annex.europe.give_to_yugoslavia"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_yugoslavia
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = yugoslavia
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:yugoslavia
					is_in_faction_with = event_target:yugoslavia
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:yugoslavia }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_croatia = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16002 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		CRO = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16002 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_croatia = yes
			}
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16002 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Albania
	id = annex.16007
	title = annexation_albania
	desc = annexation_albania
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_albania = yes }
					add_core_of = ALB
				}
				else = {
					remove_core_of = ALB
					remove_claim_by = ALB
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = GRE }
				165 = { retain_state = yes } #Northern Epirus
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Release ALB
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16007 option annex.16007.a"
		name = annex.16007.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ALB }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16007 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_albania = yes }
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16007 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_albania = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.16007 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
